5
and at the bottom its encoding as LTS.
Its six elements
x_{1},\ldots,x_{6}
give rise to the states

\vec{x}_{1},\ldots,\vec{x}_{6}
and
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{1},\ldots,\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{6}

with transitions with label
\mathtt{cv}
and
\mathtt{dr}
between them.
Since the elements
x_{1}
,
x_{2}
, and
x_{5}
satisfy proposition
letter
r
, the states
\vec{x}_{1}
,
\vec{x}_{2}
, and
\vec{x}_{5}

have a selfloop with label
r
. Similarly, the elements
x_{3}

and
x_{6}
with proposition letter
g
yield the selfloops with
label
g
for
\vec{x}_{3}
and
\vec{x}_{6}
, the element
x_{4}
with
proposition letter
b
yields the selfloop with label
b
.
As indicated by the arrow, we have that
x_{1}\in\mathcal{C}(x_{2})
. Moreover, both
x_{1}
and
x_{2}
satisfy proposition
letter
r
only. So, in the LTS we have the transitions

\vec{x}_{1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{x}_{2}
and

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{2}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{1}
.
Since
x_{2},x_{4}\in\mathcal{C}(x_{3})
and
x_{5}\in\mathcal{C}(x_{6})
and
different proposition letters are satisfied by the pairs of
elements, we have
\mathtt{ch}
-transitions
\vec{x}_{2}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{ch}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{x}_{3}
,

\vec{x}_{4}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{ch}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{x}_{3}
, and
\vec{x}_{5}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{ch}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{x}_{6}
and
their counterparts
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{3}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{ch}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{2}
,

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{3}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{ch}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{4}
, and
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{6}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{ch}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{5}
.

Below, Theorem
8
states that CoPa-bisimilarity of
two elements of a closure model
\mathcal{M}
coincides with branching
bisimilarity of their corresponding states
in
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
. In preparation of the proof the theorem
we first establish two lemmas regarding the structure
of
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
.
In turn, in aid of proving the first lemma, we introduce the notion of
the depth of a state in an LTS and note a number of properties of it.

Let a finite LTS
\mathcal{S}=(S,\mathcal{A},{\rightarrow})
and an
action
\tau\in\mathcal{A}
be given. For
s\in S
, we define the
depth
|s|
of
s
in
\mathcal{S}
by

|s|=\max\{\,{n\in\mathbb{N}}\mid{\exists\mkern 1.0mus_{0},\ldots,s_{n}\in S\colon\forall\mkern 1.0mui,\,0\leqslant i<n\colon(s_{i}\mathbin{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\!\!{}^{\ast}}s_{i{+}1})\land\neg\mkern 1.0mu(s_{i{+}1}\mathbin{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\!\!{}^{\ast}}s_{i})}\,\}

By finiteness of
S
, the above is well-defined; in the sequence
s_{0},\ldots,s_{n}
each state
s_{i}
can only occur once.

For
s,t\in S
we define
s\equiv_{\tau}t
iff

(s\mathbin{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\!\!{}^{\ast}}t)\land(t\mathbin{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\!\!{}^{\ast}}s)
. A straightforward
verification shows that the relation

{\equiv_{\tau}}\subseteq{S\times S}
is a branching bisimulation
relation on
\mathcal{S}
. Therefore, (i)
s\equiv_{\tau}t

implies
s\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[6.99997pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}t
. Also, (ii) if
|s|=|t|
and
s\mathbin{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\!\!{}^{\ast}}t

then we have
s\equiv_{\tau}t
. For, if
s\not\equiv_{\tau}t
, we have

\neg\mkern 1.0mu(t\mathbin{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\!\!{}^{\ast}}s)
and
|s|>|t|
would follow.
Thus, (iii) if
|s|=0
and
s\mathbin{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\!\!{}^{\ast}}t
it follows
that
s\equiv_{\tau}t
. Finally, from the definition of depth one
directly obtains that (iv)
s\mathbin{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\!\!{}^{\ast}}t
implies

|s|\geqslant|t|
.

Lemma 6
.

Let
\mathcal{M}=(X,\mathcal{C},\mathcal{V})
be a finite CM.
For
x\in X
, it holds in
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
that

if
\vec{x}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu
and
\vec{x}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu
then
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 1.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}
and
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 1.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu

Proof 4.1
.

For
x,x^{\prime}\in X
, if
\vec{x}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu
then

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 1.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}
by construction of
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
.
We verify the conclusion
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 1.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu
of the lemma by
induction on the depth
0pt{\vec{x}}
.

Basis,
0pt{\vec{x}}=0
:
Because
0pt{\vec{x}}=0
and
\vec{x}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu
, we
have by (iii) that
\vec{x}\equiv_{\tau}\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu
.
By construction of
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
,
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 1.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu\equiv_{\tau}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}
,
and therefore
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 1.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}
by (i).

Induction step,
0pt{\vec{x}}>0
:
Assume
\vec{x}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu
and
\vec{x}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu
.
We have
\vec{x}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{cv}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}
by construction
of
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
.
Because
\vec{x}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu
, a matching computation
for
\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu
exists,

\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu=\vec{u}_{0}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}{}\cdots\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{u}_{n}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{cv}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n}
say, where
n\geqslant 0
and

u_{0},\ldots,u_{n}\in X
are such that
\vec{u}_{i}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\vec{x}

for
0\leqslant i\leqslant n
and
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}
.
(Note that
\vec{u}_{0},\ldots,\vec{u}_{n}
rather than

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{0},\ldots,\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n}
are involved, since

\tau
-transitions preserve direction,
i.e.
, if
s\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}t

in
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
then either
s=\vec{x},t=\vec{y}
or

s=\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu},t=\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}
, for suitable
x,y\in X
.)
Since
\vec{u}_{0}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}{}\cdots\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{u}_{n}
, it holds by (iv) that

0pt{\vec{u}_{0}}\geqslant\cdots\geqslant 0pt{\vec{u}_{n}}
.
We distinguish two cases.

Case I,
0pt{\vec{u}_{0}}=0pt{\vec{u}_{n}}
:
We have
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{0}\equiv_{\tau}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n}
by (ii).
So,
\vec{u}_{0}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\vec{u}_{n}
by (i) and hence
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{0}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n}
by construction of
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
.
Using
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 1.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu=\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{0}
and
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}
it
follows that

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 1.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{0}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}
.

Case II,
0pt{\vec{u}_{0}}\neq 0pt{\vec{u}_{n}}
:
We have
0pt{\vec{u}_{0}}>0pt{\vec{u}_{n}}
by (iv).
Let
k
,
0\leqslant k<n
, be the minimal index such that

0pt{\vec{u}_{k}}>0pt{\vec{u}_{k{+}1}}
.
By hypothesis
\vec{x}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu
.
So,

0pt{\vec{x}}\geqslant 0pt{\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu}=0pt{\vec{u}_{0}}=0pt{\vec{u}_{k}}>0pt{\vec{u}_{\ell}}
for

k{+}1\leqslant\ell\leqslant n
.
By choice of
u_{n}
we have

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n}

(1)

Because
\vec{u}_{k{+}1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}{}\cdots{}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{u}_{n}

and
\vec{u}_{k{+}1}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}{}\cdots{}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\vec{u}_{n}
, it
follows by induction hypothesis that
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{k{+}1}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}{}\cdots\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}{}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n}
.
Hence,

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{k{+}1}

(2)

Since
\vec{u}_{k}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\vec{u}_{k{+}1}
and

\vec{u}_{k}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{dr}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{k}
, a matching sequence
for
\vec{u}_{k{+}1}
exists, which is, by definition
of
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
, of the form

\vec{u}_{k{+}1}=\vec{v}_{0}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}{}\cdots{}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{v}_{m}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{dr}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle v\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle v\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle v\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle v\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{m}
for suitable
m\geqslant 0
and

v_{0},\ldots,v_{m}\in X
such that

\vec{v}_{0},\ldots,\vec{v}_{m}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\vec{u}_{k}
and

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle v\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle v\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle v\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle v\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{m}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{k}
.
From
\vec{u}_{k{+}1}\mathbin{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\!\!{}^{\ast}}\vec{v}_{j}
we get, by (iv) that

0pt{\vec{u}_{k{+}1}}\geqslant 0pt{\vec{v}_{j}}
for
0\leqslant j\leqslant m
.
Moreover, we know that

0pt{\vec{x}}\geqslant 0pt{\vec{u_{0}}}=0pt{\vec{u_{k}}}>0pt{\vec{u_{k{+}1}}}
.
Thus, we get that

0pt{\vec{x}}\geqslant 0pt{\vec{u}_{0}}=0pt{\vec{u}_{k}}>0pt{\vec{u}_{k{+}1}}\geqslant 0pt{\vec{v}_{j}}
for

0\leqslant j\leqslant m
.
By induction hypothesis we obtain
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{k{+}1}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle v\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle v\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle v\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle v\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{0},\ldots,\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle v\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle v\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle v\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle v\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{m}
.
In particular,

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{k{+}1}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle v\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle v\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle v\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle v\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{m}

(3)

By choice of
v_{m}
we have

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle v\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle v\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle v\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle v\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{m}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{k}

(4)

Finally, from

0pt{\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu}=0pt{\vec{u}_{0}}=0pt{\vec{u}_{k}}
and

\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu=\vec{u}_{0}\mathbin{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\!\!{}^{\ast}}\vec{u}_{k}
one derives

\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu\equiv_{\tau}\vec{u}_{k}
using (ii).
Therefore,
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 1.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu\equiv_{\tau}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{k}
, which implies

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 1.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{k}

(5)

by (i).
Combining equations (
1
) to (
5
) gives

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{k{+}1}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle v\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle v\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle v\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle v\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{m}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern
