2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 1.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu
. So
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 1.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu
, as was to
be shown.

The next lemma states branching bisimilarity of pairs of forward
states and of backward states in an encoding LTS are related.

Lemma 7
.

Let
\mathcal{M}=(X,\mathcal{C},\mathcal{V})
be a finite CM. Then

\vec{x}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\vec{y}\Leftrightarrow\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}
in

\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
for
x,y\in X
.

Proof 4.2
.

(
\Rightarrow
) Let
x,y\in X
such that
\vec{x}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\vec{y}
.
For the transition
\vec{x}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{cv}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}
of
\vec{x}
, a matching
computation of
\vec{y}
exists, which is of the form

\vec{y}=\vec{u}_{0}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{u}_{1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}{}\cdots{}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{u}_{n}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{cv}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n}
by definition of
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
and
satisfies
\vec{u}_{0},\ldots,\vec{u}_{n}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\vec{x}
and

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}
. Repeated application of
Lemma
6
yields

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}{}\cdots{}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{0}=\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}
and

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}
. Thus
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle u\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle u\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}
,
as was to be shown.
(
\Leftarrow
) Similar to the above.

The encoding of Definition
4.1
preserves
CoPa-bisimilarity of a QdCM
\mathcal{M}
and reflects branching
bisimilarity of the LTS
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
.

Theorem 8
.

Let
\mathcal{M}=(X,\mathcal{C},\mathcal{V})
be a finite CM. For
x,y\in X
, it holds that

x\rightleftharpoons_{\mathtt{CoPa}}y
in
\mathcal{M}
iff

\vec{x}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\vec{y}
in
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})

Proof 4.3
.

(
\Leftarrow
) Define the relation
B\subseteq{X\times X}
by

x\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}y
if
\vec{x}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\vec{y}
for
x,y\in X
. We verify
that the relation
B
is a CoPa-bisimulation
using Lemma
3
.
Suppose
x,y\in X

satisfy
x\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}y
. Thus
\vec{x}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\vec{y}
.

(i) Let
p\in{\tt AP}
. If
x\in\mathcal{V}(p)
, then
\vec{x}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{x}

in
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
.
Since
\vec{x}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\vec{y}
in
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
,
exist
s,t\in S

such that
\vec{y}\mathbin{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\!\!{}^{\ast}}s\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}t
with
s,t\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\vec{x}
. By definition of
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
, it must be that

s,t=\vec{y}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu
for some
y^{\prime}\in\mathcal{V}(p)
. Moreover,

\vec{y}\mathbin{\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\!\!{}^{\ast}}\vec{y}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu
implies, again by definition
of
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
, that
y=_{\mathcal{V}}y^{\prime}
. Hence
y\in\mathcal{V}(p)
. Symmetrically,
y\in\mathcal{V}(p)
implies
x\in\mathcal{V}(p)
. So, we conclude
x=_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}y
.

(ii) Suppose
x^{\prime}\in\mathcal{C}(x)
for some
x^{\prime}\in X
.
It suffices to show that a path

(y_{i})_{i{=}0}^{n}
from
y
exists such that
x\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}y_{i}
for
0\leqslant i<n
and
x^{\prime}\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}y_{n}
. The assumption

x^{\prime}\in\mathcal{C}(x)
implies that
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
has either the
transition
\vec{x}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu
or the transition
\vec{x}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{ch}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu
.
In the case of a
\tau
-transition,
by branching bisimilarity of
\vec{x}
and
\vec{y}
,
a computation
\vec{y}_{0}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}{}\cdots{}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{y}_{n}

from
\vec{y}
with
n\geqslant 0
exists such that

\vec{x}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\vec{y}_{0},\ldots,\vec{y}_{n{-}1}

and
\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\vec{y}_{n}
.
If
n>0
, by definition of
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
, it holds that

y_{i{+}1}\in\mathcal{C}(y_{i})
for
0\leqslant i<n
.
So,
(y_{i})_{i{=}0}^{n}
is a
path from
y
in
X
as required.
If
n=0
, we have both
x\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}y
and
x^{\prime}\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}y
, and then the two
elements path
(y,y)
is a path from
y
in
X
as required.
In the case of a
\mathtt{ch}
-transition,
by branching bisimilarity of
\vec{x}

and
\vec{y}
, a computation

y_{0}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}{}\cdots{}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}y_{n{-}1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{ch}}}{{\longrightarrow}}y_{n}

from
\vec{y}
with
n\geqslant 1
exists such that

\vec{x}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}y_{0},\ldots,y_{n{-}1}
and
\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}y_{n}
.
Also here by definition of
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
,
(y_{i})_{i{=}0}^{n}
is a
path from
y
in
X
as required.

(iii) Now suppose
x\in\mathcal{C}(x^{\prime})
for some
x^{\prime}\in X
.
We have to show that a path
(y_{i})_{i{=}0}^{n}
to
y
exists such that

x^{\prime}\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}y_{0}
and
x\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}y_{i}
for
0<i\leqslant n
.
We will use that
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}
, which follows from
\vec{x}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\vec{y}
by Lemma
7
. It holds that in

\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
either the transition
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 1.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu
or the
transition
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{ch}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 1.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu
exists.
In the case of a
\tau
-transition,
by branching bisimilarity of

\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}

and

\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}

,

\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
has a computation

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{0}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}{}\cdots{}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n}
from

\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}

with
n\geqslant 0
such that

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{0},\ldots,\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n{-}1}
and

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 1.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n}
.
Lemma
7
yields

\vec{x}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\vec{y}_{0},\ldots,\vec{y}_{n{-}1}
and

\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\vec{y}_{n}
, thus
x\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}y_{i}
for
0\leqslant i<n
and

x^{\prime}\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}y_{n}
.
Note, by definition of
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
,
y_{i}\in\mathcal{C}(y_{i{+}1})
for
0\leqslant i<n
.
Therefore, if
n>0
then, in reverse order,
(y_{n{-}i})_{i{=}0}^{n}
is a
path to
y
in
X
as required.
If
n=0
, we have both
x^{\prime}\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}y
and
x\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}y
.
Consequently, the path
(y,y)
is a path to
y
in
X
satisfying
what is required.
In the case of a
\mathtt{ch}
-transition,
by branching bisimilarity of

\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}

and

\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}

, a computation

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{0}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}{}\cdots{}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n{-}1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{ch}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n}
from

\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}

with
n\geqslant 1
exists such that

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{0},\ldots,\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n{-}1}
and

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 1.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}y_{n}
.
Again we obtain
\vec{x}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\vec{y}_{0},\ldots,\vec{y}_{n{-}1}
and

\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\vec{y}_{n}
, thus
x\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}y_{i}
for
0\leqslant i<n
and

x^{\prime}\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}y_{n}
by application of Lemma
7
.
Moreover,
y_{i}\in\mathcal{C}(y_{i{+}1})
for
0\leqslant i<n
by
definition of
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
.
Thus, we have that, again in reversed order,
(y_{n{-}i})_{i{=}0}^{n}

is a path to
y
in
X
as required.

(
\Leftarrow
)
We verify that the relation
B\subseteq S\times S
given by

B=\{\,{(\vec{x},\vec{y}),(\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu},\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu})}\mid{x\rightleftharpoons_{\mathtt{CoPa}}y}\,\}
is a
branching bisimulation relation on
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
. Suppose

x,y\in X
satisfy
x\rightleftharpoons_{\mathtt{CoPa}}y
.

We check that each
transition of
\vec{x}
can be matched by
\vec{y}
.
(i) If
\vec{x}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{x}
for some proposition
p\in{\tt AP}
, then

x\in\mathcal{V}(p)
. Hence, by CoPa-bisimilarity,
y\in\mathcal{V}(p)
.
So,
\vec{y}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{y}
, and the single-step computation

\vec{y}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{y}
in
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
matches the transition

\vec{x}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{x}
.
(ii) The transition
\vec{x}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{cv}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}
is matched by the
single-step computation
\vec{y}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{cv}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}
, by construction of
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
.
(iii) If
\vec{x}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu
for some
x^{\prime}\in X
, then

x^{\prime}\in\mathcal{C}(x)
and
x=_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}x^{\prime}
. Thus,
(x,x^{\prime})
is a
path from
x
in
X
. By CoPa-bisimilarity of
x
and
y
, it
follows that in
X
a path
(y_{i})_{i{=}0}^{n}
from
y
exists
such that
x\rightleftharpoons_{\mathtt{CoPa}}y_{0},\ldots,y_{n{-}1}
and
x^{\prime}\rightleftharpoons_{\mathtt{CoPa}}y_{n}
. Then we have
x=_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}y_{0},\ldots,y_{n{-}1}

and also
x=_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}x^{\prime}=_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}y_{n}
. Thus, both

y_{i{+}1}\in\mathcal{C}(y_{i})
and
y_{i}=_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}y_{i{+}1}
for

0\leqslant i<n
. Hence, in
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
we have a computation

\vec{y}_{0}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}{}\cdots{}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{y}_{n}
such that

\vec{x}\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}\vec{y}_{i}
for
0\leqslant i<n
and

\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}\vec{y}_{n}
. Therefore,
\vec{y}
matches with this
computation the
\tau
-transition of
\vec{x}
.
(iv) Similarly, if
\vec{x}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{ch}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu
for some
x^{\prime}\in X
,
then we find in
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
a computation

\vec{y}_{0}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}{}\cdots{}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{y}_{n{-}1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{ch}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{y}_{n}
from
\vec{y}
which matches the
\mathtt{ch}
-transition of
\vec{x}
.

Next we check that each transition of

\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}

can be matched
by a computation of

\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}

.
(i) The transition
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{dr}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{x}
is matched by the
single-step computation
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{dr}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{y}
.
(ii) If
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 1.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu
for some
x^{\prime}\in X
, a reasoning
similar to the corresponding case for
\vec{x}
applies. By definition
of
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
, it holds that
x\in\mathcal{C}(x^{\prime})
and

x=_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}x^{\prime}
. Thus,
(x^{\prime},x)
is a path to
x
in
X
. By
CoPa-bisimilarity of
x
and
y
, it follows that in
X
a
path
(y_{i})_{i{=}0}^{n}
to
y
exists that satisfies
x^{\prime}\rightleftharpoons_{\mathtt{CoPa}}y_{0}

and
x\rightleftharpoons_{\mathtt{CoPa}}y_{1},\ldots,y_{n}
. Then we have
x=_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}x^{\prime}=_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}y_{0}

and also
x=_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}y_{i}
for
0<i\leqslant n
. Thus, both

y_{i{+}1}\in\mathcal{C}(y_{i})
and
y_{i}=_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}y_{i{+}1}
for

0\leqslant i<n
. Hence, by definition of
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
, a
computation
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}{}\cdots{}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{0}

of

\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}

exists which satisfies
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{i}
for

n\geqslant i>0
and
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 1.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{0}
. Therefore, this
computation of

\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}

matches the
\tau
-transition of

\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}

.
(iii) Finally, a transition
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{ch}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 1.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu
for
some
x^{\prime}\in X
is matched by

\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}

.
In fact, if
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{ch}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 1.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu
for some
x^{\prime}\in X
, then it holds that

x\in\mathcal{C}(x^{\prime})
and
x\neq_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}x^{\prime}
. Thus
(x^{\prime},x)
is a path
to
x
in
\mathcal{M}
. Let by CoPa-bisimilarity of
x
and
y
, the
path
(y_{i})_{i{=}0}^{n}
be a matching path to
y
.
Note
n\geqslant 1
as
x\not\rightleftharpoons_{\mathtt{CoPa}}x^{\prime}
. We have

x^{\prime}\rightleftharpoons_{\mathtt{CoPa}}y_{0}
and
x\rightleftharpoons_{\mathtt{CoPa}}y_{1},\ldots,y_{n}
. Thus,

y_{0}=_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}x^{\prime}\neq_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}x=_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}y_{1}
and
y_{1}=_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}\cdots=_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}y_{n}
. So,
in
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
the computation
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}{}\cdots\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{ch}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{0}
exists that
satisfies
x\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}y_{i}
for
n\geqslant i>0

and
x^{\prime}\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}y_{0}
. We see, this computation of

\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}

matches the

\mathtt{ch}
-transition of

\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}

as was to be shown.

4.2.
LTS encoding of symmetric QdCMs

For finite CMs that are symmetric a simplified version of the
encoding can be given. These CMs naturally arise as representations
of digital images where points are related via an adjacency relation
as discussed in Section
1
. Because of the
relevance these instances we discuss below a concise version of the encoding
of the previous subsection.

{defi}

[Encoding, symmetric case]

Let
\mathcal{M}=(X,\mathcal{C},\mathcal{V})
be a symmetric CM.
Define the labelled transition system

\mathtt{LTS_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{sym}}}(\mathcal{M})=(X,\mathcal{A},{\rightarrow})
by

(i)

\mathcal{A}={\tt AP}\cup\{\tau,\mathtt{ch}\}
;

(ii)

the transition relation
{\rightarrow}\subseteq{X\times\mathcal{A}\times X}
contains exactly the
following transitions:

\begin{array}[]{cl}x\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}x&\text{for $x\in X$, $p\in{\tt AP}$ with $x\in\mathcal{V}(p)$}\\
x\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}x^{\prime}&\text{if $x^{\prime}\in\mathcal{C}(x)\backslash\{x\}$ and $x=_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}x^{\prime}$}\\
x\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{ch}}}{{\longrightarrow}}x^{\prime}&\text{if $x^{\prime}\in\mathcal{C}(x)$ and $x\neq_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}x^{\prime}$}\\
\end{array}

x_{1}
\{r\}
x_{2}
\{r\}
x_{3}
\{g\}
x_{4}
\{b\}
x_{5}
\{r\}
x_{6}
\{g\}

Theorem 9
.

Let
\mathcal{M}=(X,\mathcal{C},\mathcal{V})
be a finite and symmetric CM. For

x,y\in X
it holds that

x\rightleftharpoons_{\mathtt{CoPa}}y
in
\mathcal{M}
iff

x\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}y
in
\mathtt{LTS_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{sym}}}(\mathcal{M})

Proof 4.4
.

We prove the statement
\vec{x}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}\vec{y}
in
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})

iff
x\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}y
in
\mathtt{LTS_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{sym}}}(\mathcal{M})
for
x,y\in X
. Together with
Theorem
8
, leads to the assertion.

(
\Leftarrow
) We verify that the relation
B
given by

B=\{\,{\langle\vec{x},\vec{y}\,\rangle,\,\langle\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu},\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\,\rangle}\mid{x\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}y}\,\}

is a branching bisimulation relation, where branching bisimilarity
of
x
and
y
is considered in
\mathtt{LTS_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{sym}}}(\mathcal{M})
.
Let
x,y\in X
be such that
x\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}y
. Then
\vec{x}\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}\vec{y}

and
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}
. We first analyze the transitions of
\vec{x}

in
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
.

(i)

Regarding the transition
\vec{x}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{cv}}}{{\longrightarrow}}x^{\prime}
in

\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
we note that
\vec{y}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{cv}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}
and that

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}
.

(ii)

If
\vec{x}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{y}
in
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
then

x\in\mathcal{V}(p)
for
p\in{\tt AP}
. Also,
x\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}x
in

\mathtt{LTS_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{sym}}}(\mathcal{M})
. Because
x\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}y
, we have

y=y_{0}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\cdots\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}y_{n{-}1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}y_{n}

for some
n>0
and
y_{0},\ldots,y_{n}\in X
with

x\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}y_{0},\ldots,y_{n}
.
Because
y_{n{-}1}\in\mathcal{V}(p)
it follows from the
construction of
\mathtt{LTS_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{sym}}}(\mathcal{M})
that
y_{n{-}1}=y_{n}
and

y_{n{-}1}\in\mathcal{V}(p)
. As
y_{0}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\cdots\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}y_{n{-}1}
it follows from the
construction of
\mathtt{LTS_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{sym}}}(\mathcal{M})
as well that
y_{0}=_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}\cdots=_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}y_{n{-}1}
.
We conclude that
y=y_{0}\in\mathcal{V}(p)
and therefore

\vec{y}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle p}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{y}
by construction of
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
.

(iii)

If
\vec{x}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu
in
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
for
some
x^{\prime}\in X
, then it holds that
x=x^{\prime}
, or
x^{\prime}\in\mathcal{C}(x)\backslash\{x\}
and
x=_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}x^{\prime}
.
In the first case, i.e.
x=x^{\prime}
, we have

\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu\mathop{=}\vec{x}\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}\vec{y}
.
In the second case,
x\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}x^{\prime}
in

\mathtt{LTS_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{sym}}}(\mathcal{M})
. As
x\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}y
, a computation

y=y_{0}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\cdots\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}y_{n{-}1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}y_{n}

exists in
\mathtt{LTS_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{sym}}}(\mathcal{M})
with
n\geqslant 0
,

y_{0},\ldots,y_{n{-}1}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}x
, and
y_{n}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}x^{\prime}
. We have,
without loss of generality,

y_{i{+}1}\in\mathcal{C}(y_{i})\backslash\{y_{i}\}
and

y_{i}=_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}y_{i{+}1}
for
0\leqslant i<n
. Thus,

\vec{y}=\vec{y}_{0}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\cdots\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{y}_{n{-}1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{y}_{n}
is a computation in
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
, and, by
definition of
B
, it holds that

\vec{x}\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}\vec{y}_{0},\ldots,\vec{y}_{n{-}1}
, and

\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}\vec{y}_{n}
.

(iv)

If
\vec{x}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{ch}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu
in
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
for
some
x^{\prime}\in X
, then it holds that
x^{\prime}\in\mathcal{C}(x)
and

x\neq_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}x^{\prime}
. So,
x\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{ch}}}{{\longrightarrow}}x^{\prime}
in
\mathtt{LTS_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{sym}}}(\mathcal{M})
.
As
x\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}y
, a computation

y=y_{0}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\cdots\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}y_{n{-}1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{ch}}}{{\longrightarrow}}y_{n}

exists in
\mathtt{LTS_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{sym}}}(\mathcal{M})
with
n>0
,

y_{0},\ldots,y_{n{-}1}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}x
, and
y_{n}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}x^{\prime}
. We have,
without loss of generality,

y_{i{+}1}\in\mathcal{C}(y_{i})\backslash\{y_{i}\}
for

0\leqslant i<n
and

y_{0}=_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}\cdots=_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}y_{n{-}1}\neq_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}y_{n}
. Thus,

\vec{y}=\vec{y}_{0}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\cdots\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{y}_{n{-}1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{ch}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{y}_{n}
is a computation in
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
, and, by
definition of
B
, it holds that

\vec{x}\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}\vec{y}_{0},\ldots,\vec{y}_{n{-}1}
, and

\vec{x}\mkern 2.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}\vec{y}_{n}
.

Next, we consider transitions of

\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}

in
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
.

(i)

Regarding the transition
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{dr}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}
in

\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
we note that
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{dr}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}
and

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}
.

(ii)

If
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 1.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu
in
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
for
some
x^{\prime}\in X
, then it holds that
x=x^{\prime}
, or
x\in\mathcal{C}(x^{\prime})\backslash\{x\}

and
x=_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}x^{\prime}
.
Thus,
x=x^{\prime}
or
x\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}x^{\prime}
in
\mathtt{LTS_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{sym}}}(\mathcal{M})
,
by symmetry of
\mathcal{M}
.
In case
x=x^{\prime}
we are done since

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 1.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu\mathop{=}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}
. In the other case, we
reason as follows.
Since
x\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}y
, a computation
y=y_{0}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\cdots\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}y_{n{-}1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}y_{n}

exists in
\mathtt{LTS_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{sym}}}(\mathcal{M})
with
n\geqslant 0
,

y_{0},\ldots,y_{n{-}1}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}x
, and
y_{n}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}x^{\prime}
.
From
y_{i}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}y_{i{+}1}
in
\mathtt{LTS_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{sym}}}(\mathcal{M})
, for
0\leqslant i<n
, we have, by construction of

\mathtt{LTS_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{sym}}}(\mathcal{M})
(see Definition
4.2
), that
y_{i{+}1}\in\mathcal{C}(y_{i})
. In addition, by symmetry of
\mathcal{M}
, we get that also
y_{i}\in\mathcal{C}(y_{i{+}1})
holds for
0\leqslant i<n
.
Thus, by construction of
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
(see Definition
4.1
), we get that
\vec{y}_{i{+}1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{y}_{i}

is a transition of
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
, for
0\leqslant i<n
and then

\vec{y}_{n}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{y}_{n{-}1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\cdots\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{y}_{0}
is a computation
of
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
. By construction of of
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
we also get that

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}=\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{0}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\cdots\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n{-}1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n}
is a computation in
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
.
Because
y_{0},\ldots,y_{n{-}1}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}x
and
y_{n}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}x^{\prime}
, we have

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{0},\ldots,\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n{-}1}\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}
and

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n}\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}^{\prime}
and we are done.

(iii)

If
\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{ch}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}\mkern 1.0mu{}^{\prime}\mkern-2.0mu
in
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
for
some
x^{\prime}\in X
, we proceed in a similar way as in the previous case.
It holds that
x\in\mathcal{C}(x^{\prime})
,
x\neq x^{\prime}
, and
x\neq_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathcal{V}}x^{\prime}
.
By symmetry of
\mathcal{M}
it follows
that
x^{\prime}\in\mathcal{C}(x)
. Thus,
x\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{ch}}}{{\longrightarrow}}x^{\prime}
in

\mathtt{LTS_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{sym}}}(\mathcal{M})
.

Since
x\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}y
, a computation
y=y_{0}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\cdots\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}y_{n{-}1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{ch}}}{{\longrightarrow}}y_{n}

exists in
\mathtt{LTS_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{sym}}}(\mathcal{M})
with
n\geqslant 0
,

y_{0},\ldots,y_{n{-}1}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}x
, and
y_{n}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}x^{\prime}
.
From
y_{i}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}y_{i{+}1}
in
\mathtt{LTS_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{sym}}}(\mathcal{M})
, for
0\leqslant i<n
, we have, by construction of

\mathtt{LTS_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{sym}}}(\mathcal{M})
(see Definition
4.2
), that
y_{i{+}1}\in\mathcal{C}(y_{i})
. In addition, by symmetry of
\mathcal{M}
, we get that also
y_{i}\in\mathcal{C}(y_{i{+}1})
holds for
0\leqslant i<n
.
Thus, by construction of
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
(see Definition
4.1
),
we get that
\vec{y}_{i{+}1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{y}_{i}

is a transition of
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
, for
0\leqslant i<n-1
as well as
\vec{y}_{n}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{ch}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{y}_{n{-}1}
.

Consequently,

\vec{y}_{n}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\mathtt{ch}}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{y}_{n{-}1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\cdots\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\vec{y}_{0}
is a computation
of
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
. By construction of of
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
we also get that

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}=\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{0}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\cdots\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\tau}}{{\longrightarrow}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern is a computation in
\mathtt{LTS}_{\mkern 1.0mu\mathrm{gen}}(\mathcal{M})
.
Because
y_{0},\ldots,y_{n{-}1}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}x
and
y_{n}\mathrel{\,\raisebox{1.29167pt}{$\underline{\makebox[7.15547pt]{$\leftrightarrow$}}$}\,}_{b}x^{\prime}
, we have

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{0},\ldots,\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n{-}1}\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}
and

\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle y\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle y\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}_{n}\mathop{\mkern-2.0mu\textit{B}}\mathchoice{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\displaystyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\displaystyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 3.0mu\reflectbox{$\textstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-3.0mu\textstyle x\mkern 3.0mu$}}$}\mkern-3.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}{\mkern 2.0mu\reflectbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\vec{\reflectbox{$\mkern-2.0mu\scriptscriptstyle x\mkern 2.0mu$}}$}\mkern-2.0mu}^{\prime}
and we are done.

This finishes the proof from right to left.

) We verify that the relation

Figure 11.
An image of a 2D maze, with green exit (upper-left), blue starting points, black walls and white walking areas (Figure

For readability, self-loops labelled by atomic propositions are not shown; the corresponding states are shown in the colour represented by the omitted label; symmetric transition pairs are drawn as doubly-headed arrows.

The 2D maze in Figure
11a
, which will also be part of our feasibility study in Section
5
, exemplifies the significance of CoPa-minimization on images. Each state of the LTS represents an area of interest in the image: exit (green), walls (black), walking areas (white) and starting points (blue). The three white states, as an example, represent three different kinds of white walking areas: the ones from which neither an exit nor a starting point can be reached (without crossing walls), the ones from which a starting point can be reached (but not the exit), and the ones from which a starting point and the exit can be reached.

5.
Spatial Model Checking of Digital Images via Minimised Models

In this section we present the
VoxMinX
toolchain for spatial model checking of digital images, that exploits the minimisation procedure described in Section
4
. We first present a high-level overview of the toolchain, followed by a more detailed discussion in subsequent subsections. We then describe an experimental evaluation showing the feasibility of the proposed toolchain and compare its results with that of the spatial model checker
VoxLogicA
for a selected set of benchmark images.
